{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01R9VhQspufxnXcDLctypGs1","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→#!/usr/bin/env python3\n     2→\"\"\"\n     3→Convert the code for experience relative\n     7→                   to reward\", Nature Neuroscience 2025.\n     8→Reference code:   /app/code  (GiocomoLab/Sosa_et_al_2024)\n     9→\n    10→Usage:\n    11→ \"C\": (320.0, 370.0)}\n    40→ZONE_NAMES = [\"A\", \"B\", \"C\"]\n    41→\n    42→# reward_relative/behavior.py :: get_reward_zones(..., change_trial=30)\n    43→# Paper: \"Each switch occurred after 30 trials.\"\n    44→CHANGE_TRIAL = 30\n    45→\n    46→# reward_relative/utilities.py :: default_dff_method\n    47→NEU_COEF = 0.7                 # neuropil subtraction coefficient\n    48→BASELINE_FILTER_LEN = 300      # maximin window, 300 samples @15.5 Hz ~= 19.3 s (\"20 s\")\n    49→BASELINE_SMOOTH_SIGMA = 15     # pre-maximin Gaussian smoothing sigma (samples)\n    50→DFF_SMOOTH_SIGMA = 2           # post-dF/F Gaussian smoothing sigma (~0.129 s)\n    51→\n    52→# Paper: putative interneurons excluded if corr(dF/F, speed) > 0.5\n    53→SPEED_CORR_THRESH = 0.5\n    54→\n    55→# Paper: lick-sensor failure detection (~0.65% of trials, n = 81 / 12,376)\n    56→LICK_ERR_COUNT_THRESH = 2      # cumulative lick count per imaging frame\n    57→LICK_ERR_FRAC_THRESH = 0.3     # fraction of frames in the trial\n    58→\n    59→TRACK_LENGTH = 450.0           # cm\n    60→POS_BIN_EDGES = [90.0, 180.0, 270.0, 360.0]\n    61→SPEED_BIN_EDGES = [2.0, 10.0, 20.0, 40.0]\n    62→\n    63→OUTPUT_NAMES = [\n    64→    \"reward_zone_distance\",\n    65→    \"position\",\n    66→    \"speed\",\n    67→    \"lick\",\n    68→    \"reward_zone_location\",\n    69→    \"reward_outcome\",\n    70→]\n    71→OUTPUT_VALUES = [\n    72→    [\"< -50 cm\", \"-50 to -10 cm\", \"-10 to <0 cm\", \"0 cm (in reward zone)\",\n    73→     \">0 to +10 cm\", \"+10 to +50 cm\", \"> +50 cm\"],\n    74→    [\"< 90 cm\", \"90-180 cm\", \"180-270 cm\", \"270-360 cm\", \"> 360 cm\"],\n    75→    [\"< 2 cm/s\", \"2-10 cm/s\", \"10-20 cm/s\", \"20-40 cm/s\", \"> 40 cm/s\"],\n    76→    [\"no lick\", \"lick\"],\n    77→    [\"zone A (80-130 cm)\", \"zone B (200-250 cm)\", \"zone C (320-370 cm)\"],\n    78→    [\"omitted\", \"rewarded\"],\n    79→]\n    80→INPUT_NAMES = [\n    81→    \"time_from_trial_start_s\",\n    82→    \"environment\",          # 0 = ENV1, 1 = ENV2\n    83→    \"trial_number\",\n    84→    \"previous_trial_reward\",\n    85→]\n    86→\n    87→\n    88→# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n    89→# Helpers copied / adapted from the reference code\n    90→# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n    91→\n    92→def nansmooth(a, sig, axis=-1):\n    93→    \"\"\"Gaussian smoothing that does not propagate NaNs.\n    94→\n    95→    Verbatim port of reward_relative/utilities.py :: nansmooth (and the\n    96→    equivalent TwoPUtils.utilities.nansmooth used for the dF/F baseline).\n    97→    \"\"\"\n    98→    nan_inds = np.isnan(a)\n    99→    a_nanless = np.copy(a)\n   100→    a_nanless[nan_inds] = 0\n   101→    one = np.ones(a.shape)\n   102→    one[nan_inds] = 0.001\n   103→    a_nanless = sp.ndimage.gaussian_filter1d(a_nanless, sig, axis=axis)\n   104→    one = sp.ndimage.gaussian_filter1d(one, sig, axis=axis)\n   105→    return a_nanless / one\n   106→\n   107→\n   108→def compute_dff(F, Fneu, trial_starts, trial_ends):\n   109→    \"\"\"Compute dF/F exactly as in reward_relative/preprocessing.py :: dff().\n   110→\n   111→    Steps (keep_teleports=False, neuropil_method='subtract',\n   112→    baseline_method='maximin', subtract_baseline=True):\n   113→      1. keep only within-trial samples (everything else NaN)\n   114→      2. F  <- F - 0.7 * Fneu\n   115→      3. add back 0.7 * <Fneu> of the trial so dF/F is close to true dF/F\n   116→      4. baseline = maximin (min then max filter, 300 samples ~ 20 s) of the\n   117→         Gaussian-smoothed (sigma 15) signal, computed within each trial\n   118→      5. dF/F = (F - baseline) / |baseline|\n   119→      6. smooth dF/F with a 2-sample s.d. Gaussian kernel, within each trial\n   120→\n   121→    Args:\n   122→        F:     (n_neurons, n_frames) ROI fluorescence\n   123→        Fneu:  (n_neurons, n_frames) neuropil fluorescence\n   124→        trial_starts, trial_ends: frame indices, trial i spans [start, end)\n   125→\n   126→    Returns:\n   127→        dff: (n_neurons, n_frames) with NaN outside of trials\n   128→        in_trial: boolean (n_frames,) mask of within-trial samples\n   129→    \"\"\"\n   130→    f_ = np.full(F.shape, np.nan)\n   131→    fneu_ = np.full(F.shape, np.nan)\n   132→    for s, e in zip(trial_starts, trial_ends):\n   133→        f_[:, s:e] = F[:, s:e]\n   134→        fneu_[:, s:e] = Fneu[:, s:e]\n   135→\n   136→    in_trial = ~np.isnan(f_[0, :])\n   137→\n   138→    # neuropil subtraction\n   139→    f_ -= NEU_COEF * fneu_\n   140→\n   141→    flow = np.full(F.shape, np.nan)\n   142→    for s, e in zip(trial_starts, trial_ends):\n   143→        # add back the trial-mean neuropil so we never divide by a small number\n   144→        f_[:, s:e] = f_[:, s:e] + NEU_COEF * np.nanmean(fneu_[:, s:e], axis=1,\n   145→                                                        keepdims=True)\n   146→        tmp = nansmooth(f_[:, s:e], BASELINE_SMOOTH_SIGMA, axis=1)\n   147→        tmp = sp.ndimage.minimum_filter1d(tmp, BASELINE_FILTER_LEN, axis=-1)\n   148→        flow[:, s:e] = sp.ndimage.maximum_filter1d(tmp, BASELINE_FILTER_LEN, axis=-1)\n   149→\n   150→    dff = np.full(F.shape, np.nan)\n   151→    dff[:, in_trial] = ((f_[:, in_trial] - flow[:, in_trial])\n   152→                        / np.abs(flow[:, in_trial]))\n   153→\n   154→    for s, e in zip(trial_starts, trial_ends):\n   155→        dff[:, s:e] = nansmooth(dff[:, s:e], DFF_SMOOTH_SIGMA, axis=1)\n   156→\n   157→    return dff, in_trial\n   158→\n   159→\n   160→def reward_zone_labels_from_scene(scene, n_trials, change_trial=CHANGE_TRIAL):\n   161→    \"\"\"Per-trial reward-zone label, following behavior.py :: get_reward_zones().\n   162→\n   163→    Scene strings look like 'Env1_LocationA'          (no switch)\n   164→                            'Env1_LocationB_to_A'      (zone switch)\n   165→                            'Env1_B_to_Env2_C'         (zone + environment switch)\n   166→    The zone switches after `change_trial` trials.\n   167→\n   168→    Returns list of 'A'/'B'/'C' of length n_trials.\n   169→    \"\"\"\n   170→    # no-switch scenes: Env{1,2,3}_Location{A,B,C}\n   171→    for z in ZONE_NAMES:\n   172→        if scene.endswith(\"_Location\" + z):\n   173→            return [z] * n_trials\n   174→\n   175→    # switch scenes: '<zone0>_to' ... ends with '<zone1>'\n   176→    zone1 = scene[-1]\n   177→    zone0 = None\n   178→    for z in ZONE_NAMES:\n   179→        if (z + \"_to\") in scene:\n   180→            zone0 = z\n   181→            break\n   182→    if zone0 is None or zone1 not in ZONE_NAMES:\n   183→        raise NotImplementedError(f\"Cannot parse reward zones for scene '{scene}'\")\n   184→    return [zone0] * min(change_trial, n_trials) + \\\n   185→           [zone1] * max(n_trials - change_trial, 0)\n   186→\n   187→\n   188→def discretize_reward_distance(pos, zone_start, zone_end):\n   189→    \"\"\"Signed distance to the nearest point of the reward zone -> 7 classes.\n   190→\n   191→    d < 0   : animal is before the zone (d = pos - zone_start)\n   192→    d = 0   : animal is inside the zone\n   193→    d > 0   : animal is past the zone (d = pos - zone_end)\n   194→    \"\"\"\n   195→    d = np.zeros_like(pos)\n   196→    before = pos < zone_start\n   197→    after = pos > zone_end\n   198→    d[before] = pos[before] - zone_start\n   199→    d[after] = pos[after] - zone_end\n   200→\n   201→    out = np.full(pos.shape, 3, dtype=np.int64)     # 3 == inside the zone\n   202→    out[d < -50.0] = 0\n   203→    out[(d >= -50.0) & (d < -10.0)] = 1\n   204→    out[(d >= -10.0) & (d < 0.0)] = 2\n   205→    out[(d > 0.0) & (d <= 10.0)] = 4\n   206→    out[(d > 10.0) & (d <= 50.0)] = 5\n   207→    out[d > 50.0] = 6\n   208→    return out, d\n   209→\n   210→\n   211→# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n   212→# Session processing\n   213→# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n   214→\n   215→def load_session(path):\n   216→    \"\"\"Read everything we need out of one NWB file using pynwb.\"\"\"\n   217→    from pynwb import NWBHDF5IO\n   218→\n   219→    with NWBHDF5IO(path, \"r\", load_namespaces=True) as io:\n   220→        nwb = io.read()\n   221→\n   222→        subject = nwb.subject.subject_id\n   223→        session_id = nwb.session_id\n   224→        # nwb.identifier == '/data/<...>/<animal>/<date>/<scene>'\n   225→        ident_parts = nwb.identifier.rstrip(\"/\").split(\"/\")\n   226→        scene = ident_parts[-1]\n   227→        date = ident_parts[-2]\n   228→\n   229→        ophys = nwb.processing[\"ophys\"]\n   230→        plane_seg = (ophys.data_interfaces[\"ImageSegmentation\"]\n   231→                     .plane_segmentations[\"PlaneSegmentation\"])\n   232→        # suite2p iscell.npy, column 0 == (manually curated) cell/not-cell label\n   233→        iscell = np.asarray(plane_seg[\"iscell\"].data)[:, 0].astype(bool)\n   234→        plane_idx_all = np.asarray(plane_seg[\"planeIdx\"].data).astype(int)\n   235→\n   236→        # Multi-plane animals (m17, m18) store one RoiResponseSeries per imaging\n   237→        # plane; the PlaneSegmentation table concatenates the planes' ROIs. Planes\n   238→        # are pooled, as in the paper (\"planes were pooled for all analyses except\n   239→        # those in Extended Data Fig. 7\").\n   240→        fluo = ophys.data_interfaces[\"Fluorescence\"].roi_response_series\n   241→        neuro = ophys.data_interfaces[\"Neuropil\"].roi_response_series\n   242→        plane_keys = sorted(fluo.keys(), key=lambda k: int(k.replace(\"plane\", \"\")))\n   243→        n_roi_total = len(plane_idx_all)\n   244→        n_frames = fluo[plane_keys[0]].data.shape[0]\n   245→        F = np.empty((n_roi_total, n_frames), dtype=np.float64)\n   246→        Fneu = np.empty((n_roi_total, n_frames), dtype=np.float64)\n   247→        for k in plane_keys:\n   248→            p = int(k.replace(\"plane\", \"\"))\n   249→            rows = np.where(plane_idx_all == p)[0]\n   250→            assert len(rows) == fluo[k].data.shape[1], (\n   251→                f\"{k}: {len(rows)} ROIs in table vs {fluo[k].data.shape[1]} traces\")\n   252→            F[rows] = np.asarray(fluo[k].data[:]).T\n   253→            Fneu[rows] = np.asarray(neuro[k].data[:]).T\n   254→        rate = float(fluo[plane_keys[0]].rate)\n   255→        plane_idx = plane_idx_all[iscell]\n   256→        F = F[iscell]\n   257→        Fneu = Fneu[iscell]\n   258→\n   259→        bts = (nwb.processing[\"behavior\"]\n   260→               .data_interfaces[\"BehavioralTimeSeries\"].time_series)\n   261→        beh = {k: np.asarray(v.data[:]) for k, v in bts.items() if k != \"Reward\"}\n   262→        timestamps = np.asarray(bts[\"position\"].timestamps[:])\n   263→        reward_times = np.asarray(bts[\"Reward\"].timestamps[:])\n   264→\n   265→    n_planes = int(plane_idx_all.max()) + 1\n   266→\n   267→    # In 10 of the 2-plane sessions the fluorescence series has exactly one frame\n   268→    # more than the VR-aligned behaviour: TwoPUtils' vr_align_to_2P builds\n   269→    # int(max_idx / n_planes) rows, which truncates the final volume. The extra\n   270→    # frame is at the end of the recording, so drop it.\n   271→    n_extra = F.shape[1] - len(timestamps)\n   272→    assert 0 <= n_extra <= 1, (\n   273→        f\"fluorescence has {F.shape[1]} frames, behaviour {len(timestamps)}\")\n   274→    if n_extra:\n   275→        F = F[:, :len(timestamps)]\n   276→        Fneu = Fneu[:, :len(timestamps)]\n   277→    return dict(subject=subject, session_id=session_id, scene=scene, date=date,\n   278→                iscell=iscell, plane_idx=plane_idx, F=F, Fneu=Fneu, rate=rate,\n   279→                beh=beh, timestamps=timestamps, reward_times=reward_times,\n   280→                n_planes=n_planes, n_roi_total=int(n_roi_total))\n   281→\n   282→\n   283→def process_session(path, show_processing=False, plot_prefix=None, verbose=True):\n   284→    \"\"\"Convert one NWB session into (neural, input, output, info) lists.\"\"\"\n   285→    t_load = time.time()\n   286→    S = load_session(path)\n   287→    t_load = time.time() - t_load\n   288→\n   289→    t_proc = time.time()\n   290→    beh = S[\"beh\"]\n   291→    ts = S[\"timestamps\"]\n   292→    pos = beh[\"position\"]\n   293→    speed = beh[\"speed\"]\n   294→    lick = beh[\"lick\"]\n   295→    env_ts = beh[\"environment\"]\n   296→    rzone_ts = beh[\"reward_zone\"]\n   297→    scanning = beh[\"scanning\"]\n   298→    trial_num_ts = beh[\"trial number\"]\n   299→\n   300→    starts = np.where(beh[\"trial_start\"] > 0)[0]\n   301→    ends = np.where(beh[\"teleport\"] > 0)[0]\n   302→    assert len(starts) == len(ends), \"trial_start / teleport count mismatch\"\n   303→    assert np.all(ends > starts), \"teleport must follow its trial_start\"\n   304→    n_trials_raw = len(starts)\n   305→\n   306→    # ---------------- neural: dF/F, then interneuron exclusion --------------- #\n   307→    dff, in_trial = compute_dff(S[\"F\"], S[\"Fneu\"], starts, ends)\n   308→\n   309→    # Paper: \"Additional putative interneurons were detected for exclusion ...\n   310→    #         by a Pearson correlation of >0.5 between their dF/F timeseries and\n   311→    #         the animal's running speed\"\n   312→    d = dff[:, in_trial]\n   313→    sp_ = speed[in_trial]\n   314→    d0 = d - d.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)\n   315→    s0 = sp_ - sp_.mean()\n   316→    denom = np.sqrt((d0 ** 2).sum(axis=1) * (s0 ** 2).sum())\n   317→    with np.errstate(invalid=\"ignore\", divide=\"ignore\"):\n   318→        speed_corr = (d0 @ s0) / denom\n   319→    speed_corr = np.nan_to_num(speed_corr, nan=0.0)\n   320→    keep_cells = speed_corr <= SPEED_CORR_THRESH\n   321→    n_interneuron = int((~keep_cells).sum())\n   322→    dff_all = dff\n   323→    dff = dff[keep_cells]\n   324→    plane_idx = S[\"plane_idx\"][keep_cells]\n   325→    n_neurons = dff.shape[0]\n   326→\n   327→    # ---------------- per-trial behavioural variables ------------------------ #\n   328→    # reward delivery: the 'Reward' TimeSeries holds one timestamp per delivery\n   329→    rew_frames = np.searchsorted(ts, S[\"reward_times\"])\n   330→\n   331→    zone_labels = reward_zone_labels_from_scene(S[\"scene\"], n_trials_raw)\n   332→\n   333→    trial_rewarded = np.zeros(n_trials_raw, dtype=np.int64)\n   334→    trial_env = np.zeros(n_trials_raw, dtype=np.int64)\n   335→    trial_lick_error = np.zeros(n_trials_raw, dtype=bool)\n   336→    trial_unscanned = np.zeros(n_trials_raw, dtype=bool)\n   337→    observed_zone = np.full(n_trials_raw, -1, dtype=np.int64)\n   338→\n   339→    for i, (s, e) in enumerate(zip(starts, ends)):\n   340→        # reference behavior.py :: get_trial_types\n   341→        #   isreward = any(reward > 0) and any(rzone > 0) within the trial\n   342→        in_zone = rzone_ts[s:e] > 0\n   343→        got_reward = np.any((rew_frames >= s) & (rew_frames < e))\n   344→        trial_rewarded[i] = int(got_reward and np.any(in_zone))\n   345→\n   346→        ev = np.unique(env_ts[s:e])\n   347→        ev = ev[ev >= 0]\n   348→        trial_env[i] = int(ev[0]) if ev.size else -1\n   349→\n   350→        # lick-sensor failure, exactly as described in the paper\n   351→        trial_lick_error[i] = (np.mean(lick[s:e] > LICK_ERR_COUNT_THRESH)\n   352→                               > LICK_ERR_FRAC_THRESH)\n   353→        trial_unscanned[i] = np.any(scanning[s:e] < 0)\n   354→\n   355→        if np.any(in_zone):\n   356→            p = pos[s:e][np.where(in_zone)[0][0]]\n   357→            observed_zone[i] = int(np.argmin([abs(p - REWARD_ZONES[z][0])\n   358→                                              for z in ZONE_NAMES]))\n   359→\n   360→    # sanity check: where the animal actually triggered the reward zone, the\n   361→    # zone derived from the scene name must agree\n   362→    scene_zone = np.array([ZONE_NAMES.index(z) for z in zone_labels])\n   363→    seen = observed_zone >= 0\n   364→    n_zone_mismatch = int(np.sum(observed_zone[seen] != scene_zone[seen]))\n   365→\n   366→    # previous-trial outcome. Trial 0 of a session is preceded (within minutes)\n   367→    # by ~30 rewarded warm-up trials of the same task, so it is assigned 1.\n   368→    prev_rewarded = np.empty(n_trials_raw, dtype=np.int64)\n   369→    prev_rewarded[0] = 1\n   370→    prev_rewarded[1:] = trial_rewarded[:-1]\n   371→\n   372→    # ---------------- trial curation ---------------------------------------- #\n   373→    trial_len = ends - starts\n   374→    too_short = trial_len < 2\n   375→    keep_trial = ~(trial_lick_error | trial_unscanned | too_short)\n   376→\n   377→    # ---------------- build per-trial arrays -------------------------------- #\n   378→    neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials = [], [], []\n   379→    kept_idx = []\n   380→    for i in np.where(keep_trial)[0]:\n   381→        s, e = starts[i], ends[i]\n   382→        T = e - s\n   383→\n   384→        neural_trials.append(np.ascontiguousarray(dff[:, s:e], dtype=np.float32))\n   385→\n   386→        inp = np.empty((4, T), dtype=np.float32)\n   387→        inp[0] = ts[s:e] - ts[s]           # time from trial start (s)\n   388→        inp[1] = trial_env[i]              # 0 = ENV1, 1 = ENV2\n   389→        inp[2] = i                         # trial number within session\n   390→        inp[3] = prev_rewarded[i]\n   391→        input_trials.append(inp)\n   392→\n   393→        zs, ze = REWARD_ZONES[zone_labels[i]]\n   394→        p = pos[s:e]\n   395→        out = np.empty((6, T), dtype=np.int64)\n   396→        out[0], _ = discretize_reward_distance(p, zs, ze)\n   397→        out[1] = np.clip(np.digitize(p, POS_BIN_EDGES), 0, 4)\n   398→        out[2] = np.clip(np.digitize(speed[s:e], SPEED_BIN_EDGES), 0, 4)\n   399→        out[3] = (lick[s:e] > 0).astype(np.int64)\n   400→        out[4] = scene_zone[i]\n   401→        out[5] = trial_rewarded[i]\n   402→        output_trials.append(out)\n   403→        kept_idx.append(int(i))\n   404→\n   405→    info = dict(\n   406→        file=os.path.basename(path), subject=S[\"subject\"], session_id=S[\"session_id\"],\n   407→        exp_day=int(S[\"session_id\"]), scene=S[\"scene\"], date=S[\"date\"],\n   408→        n_roi_total=S[\"n_roi_total\"], n_iscell=int(S[\"iscell\"].sum()),\n   409→        n_interneuron_excluded=n_interneuron, n_neurons=n_neurons,\n   410→        n_planes=S[\"n_planes\"], rate=S[\"rate\"],\n   411→        n_trials_raw=n_trials_raw, n_trials_kept=len(kept_idx),\n   412→        n_lick_error=int(trial_lick_error.sum()),\n   413→        n_unscanned=int(trial_unscanned.sum()), n_too_short=int(too_short.sum()),\n   414→        n_zone_mismatch=n_zone_mismatch, n_zone_observed=int(seen.sum()),\n   415→        frac_rewarded=float(trial_rewarded.mean()),\n   416→        kept_trial_idx=kept_idx,\n   417→        zone_labels=[zone_labels[i] for i in kept_idx],\n   418→        dt=float(np.median(np.diff(ts))),\n   419→    )\n   420→    t_proc = time.time() - t_proc\n   421→    info[\"t_load\"] = t_load\n   422→    info[\"t_proc\"] = t_proc\n   423→\n   424→    if verbose:\n   425→        print(f\"  {info['file']}: {n_neurons} neurons \"\n   426→              f\"({info['n_iscell']} iscell - {n_interneuron} interneuron), \"\n   427→              f\"{len(kept_idx)}/{n_trials_raw} trials, scene={S['scene']}, \"\n   428→              f\"zone-mismatch={n_zone_mismatch}/{info['n_zone_observed']}, \"\n   429→              f\"load={t_load:.1f}s proc={t_proc:.1f}s\", flush=True)\n   430→\n   431→    if show_processing:\n   432→        make_processing_plots(S, dff_all, keep_cells, speed_corr, starts, ends,\n   433→                              zone_labels, scene_zone, trial_rewarded, trial_env,\n   434→                              neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials,\n   435→                              kept_idx, plot_prefix)\n   436→\n   437→    return neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials, plane_idx, info\n   438→\n   439→\n   440→# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n   441→# Diagnostic plots\n   442→# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n   443→\n   444→def make_processing_plots(S, dff, keep_cells, speed_corr, starts, ends,\n   445→                          zone_labels, scene_zone, trial_rewarded, trial_env,\n   446→                          neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials,\n   447→                          kept_idx, prefix):\n   448→    import matplotlib\n   449→    matplotlib.use(\"Agg\")\n   450→    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n   451→\n   452→    beh, ts = S[\"beh\"], S[\"timestamps\"]\n   453→    pos, speed, lick = beh[\"position\"], beh[\"speed\"], beh[\"lick\"]\n   454→\n   455→    ntr_show = min(6, len(starts))\n   456→    i0, i1 = starts[0], ends[ntr_show - 1]\n   457→    sl = slice(i0, i1)\n   458→    t = ts[sl]\n   459→\n   460→    fig, ax = plt.subplots(9, 1, figsize=(16, 24), sharex=True)\n   461→\n   462→    # (1) raw fluorescence + neuropil for 3 example cells\n   463→    cells = np.arange(min(3, S[\"F\"].shape[0]))\n   464→    for c in cells:\n   465→        ax[0].plot(t, S[\"F\"][c, sl], lw=0.6, label=f\"F cell {c}\")\n   466→        ax[0].plot(t, S[\"Fneu\"][c, sl], lw=0.6, ls=\":\", label=f\"Fneu cell {c}\")\n   467→    ax[0].set_ylabel(\"raw F / Fneu\")\n   468→    ax[0].legend(fontsize=6, ncol=2)\n   469→    ax[0].set_title(f\"{S['subject']} ses-{S['session_id']} ({S['scene']}) \"\n   470→                    f\"- first {ntr_show} trials\\nStep 1: raw suite2p traces \"\n   471→                    f\"(iscell ROIs only)\")\n   472→\n   473→    # (2) dF/F for the same cells\n   474→    for c in cells:\n   475→        ax[1].plot(t, dff[c, sl], lw=0.7, label=f\"dF/F cell {c}\")\n   476→    ax[1].set_ylabel(\"dF/F\")\n   477→    ax[1].legend(fontsize=6, ncol=3)\n   478→    ax[1].set_title(\"Step 2: dF/F (neuropil-subtracted, maximin baseline, \"\n   479→                    \"2-sample Gaussian). NaN between trials.\")\n   480→\n   481→    # (3) population raster of the kept neurons\n   482→    kept = dff[keep_cells]\n   483→    ax[2].imshow(kept[:, sl], aspect=\"auto\", origin=\"lower\",\n   484→                 extent=[t[0], t[-1], 0, kept.shape[0]],\n   485→                 vmin=0, vmax=np.nanpercentile(kept[:, sl], 99), cmap=\"magma\")\n   486→    ax[2].set_ylabel(\"neuron\")\n   487→    ax[2].set_title(f\"Step 3: dF/F population, {kept.shape[0]} neurons kept \"\n   488→                    f\"({int((~keep_cells).sum())} excluded, speed corr > 0.5)\")\n   489→\n   490→    # (4) position + reward zone\n   491→    ax[3].plot(t, pos[sl], \"k\", lw=1)\n   492→    for j in range(ntr_show):\n   493→        s, e = starts[j], ends[j]\n   494→        zs, ze = REWARD_ZONES[zone_labels[j]]\n   495→        ax[3].fill_between([ts[s], ts[e - 1]], zs, ze, color=\"g\", alpha=0.25)\n   496→        ax[3].axvline(ts[s], color=\"b\", lw=1)\n   497→        ax[3].axvline(ts[e - 1], color=\"r\", lw=1)\n   498→        ax[3].text(ts[s], 460, f\"t{j} {zone_labels[j]}\"\n   499→                               f\"{' R' if trial_rewarded[j] else ' omit'}\",\n   500→                   fontsize=7)\n   501→    ax[3].set_ylabel(\"position (cm)\")\n   502→    ax[3].set_title(\"Step 4: position, reward zone (green), trial start (blue) \"\n   503→                    \"/ teleport (red)\")\n   504→\n   505→    # (5) discretized position\n   506→    ax[4].step(t, np.clip(np.digitize(pos[sl], POS_BIN_EDGES), 0, 4), where=\"post\")\n   507→    ax[4].set_ylabel(\"position bin\")\n   508→    ax[4].set_title(\"Step 5: discretized position (5 bins of 90 cm)\")\n   509→\n   510→    # (6) distance to reward zone (continuous + discretized)\n   511→    dist = np.full(t.shape, np.nan)\n   512→    dcls = np.full(t.shape, np.nan)\n   513→    for j in range(ntr_show):\n   514→        s, e = starts[j], ends[j]\n   515→        zs, ze = REWARD_ZONES[zone_labels[j]]\n   516→        cls, dd = discretize_reward_distance(pos[s:e], zs, ze)\n   517→        dist[s - i0:e - i0] = dd\n   518→        dcls[s - i0:e - i0] = cls\n   519→    ax[5].plot(t, dist, \"k\", lw=1)\n   520→    for y in [-50, -10, 0, 10, 50]:\n   521→        ax[5].axhline(y, color=\"gray\", lw=0.5, ls=\"--\")\n   522→    ax[5].set_ylabel(\"dist to RZ (cm)\")\n   523→    ax[5].set_title(\"Step 6: signed distance to nearest point of reward zone\")\n   524→    ax[6].step(t, dcls, where=\"post\")\n   525→    ax[6].set_ylabel(\"RZ dist bin\")\n   526→    ax[6].set_title(\"Step 7: discretized reward-zone distance (7 bins)\")\n   527→\n   528→    # (8) speed\n   529→    ax[7].plot(t, speed[sl], \"k\", lw=0.8)\n   530→    for y in SPEED_BIN_EDGES:\n   531→        ax[7].axhline(y, color=\"gray\", lw=0.5, ls=\"--\")\n   532→    ax2 = ax[7].twinx()\n   533→    ax2.step(t, np.clip(np.digitize(speed[sl], SPEED_BIN_EDGES), 0, 4),\n   534→             where=\"post\", color=\"C1\", lw=0.8)\n   535→    ax2.set_ylabel(\"speed bin\", color=\"C1\")\n   536→    ax[7].set_ylabel(\"speed (cm/s)\")\n   537→    ax[7].set_title(\"Step 8: speed and its discretization\")\n   538→\n   539→    # (9) licks\n   540→    ax[8].step(t, lick[sl], where=\"post\", color=\"k\", lw=0.8, label=\"lick count\")\n   541→    ax[8].step(t, (lick[sl] > 0).astype(float), where=\"post\", color=\"C3\", lw=0.8,\n   542→               label=\"lick binary (output)\")\n   543→    ax[8].set_ylabel(\"licks\")\n   544→    ax[8].set_xlabel(\"time (s)\")\n   545→    ax[8].legend(fontsize=7)\n   546→    ax[8].set_title(\"Step 9: lick counts per imaging frame -> binary output\")\n   547→\n   548→    fig.tight_layout()\n   549→    fig.savefig(f\"{prefix}.png\", dpi=110)\n   550→    plt.close(fig)\n   551→\n   552→    # --- second figure: final per-trial arrays, to prove alignment ---------- #\n   553→    fig, ax = plt.subplots(4, 4, figsize=(20, 12))\n   554→    for k in range(min(4, len(neural_trials))):\n   555→        n, inp, out = neural_trials[k], input_trials[k], output_trials[k]\n   556→        tt = inp[0]\n   557→        ax[0, k].imshow(n, aspect=\"auto\", origin=\"lower\",\n   558→                        extent=[tt[0], tt[-1], 0, n.shape[0]],\n   559→                        vmin=0, vmax=np.percentile(n, 99), cmap=\"magma\")\n   560→        ax[0, k].set_title(f\"trial {kept_idx[k]} dF/F ({n.shape[0]} neurons)\")\n   561→        for r in range(4):\n   562→            ax[1, k].plot(tt, inp[r], label=INPUT_NAMES[r])\n   563→        ax[1, k].legend(fontsize=6)\n   564→        ax[1, k].set_title(\"inputs\")\n   565→        for r in range(4):\n   566→            ax[2, k].step(tt, out[r], where=\"post\", label=OUTPUT_NAMES[r])\n   567→        ax[2, k].legend(fontsize=6)\n   568→        ax[2, k].set_title(\"time-varying outputs\")\n   569→        ax[3, k].step(tt, out[4], where=\"post\", label=OUTPUT_NAMES[4])\n   570→        ax[3, k].step(tt, out[5], where=\"post\", label=OUTPUT_NAMES[5])\n   571→        ax[3, k].legend(fontsize=6)\n   572→        ax[3, k].set_xlabel(\"time from trial start (s)\")\n   573→        ax[3, k].set_title(\"per-trial outputs\")\n   574→    fig.suptitle(f\"{S['subject']} ses-{S['session_id']} - converted trials\")\n   575→    fig.tight_layout()\n   576→    fig.savefig(f\"{prefix}_trials.png\", dpi=110)\n   577→    plt.close(fig)\n   578→\n   579→\n   580→# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n   581→# Driver\n   582→# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n   583→\n   584→def _worker(args):\n   585→    path, show, prefix = args\n   586→    return process_session(path, show_processing=show, plot_prefix=prefix)\n   587→\n   588→\n   589→def main():\n   590→    ap = python3\n\"\"\"\nConvert the Sosa, Plitt & Giocomo (2025) hippocampal CA1 VR dataset (DANDI:001361)\nfrom NWB into the decoder-ready pickle format.\n\nReference paper:  \"A flexible hippocampal <outpicklefile> [--full | --sample] [--show-processing]\n\n    --full             process all sessions (default)\n    --sample           process only 2 sessions (for testing)\n    --show-processing  save per-step diagnostic plots for up to 2 sessions\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport glob\nimport os\nimport pickle\nimport sys\nimport time\nimport warnings\nfrom concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport scipy as sp\nimport scipy.ndimage\n\nwarnings.filterwarnings(\"ignore\")\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# Constants taken from the reference code / paper\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n# reward_relative/behavior.py :: reward_zone_dict  (map_labels A->X, B->Y, C->Z)\n# Paper: \"zone A, 80-130 cm; zone B, 200-250 cm; zone C, 320-370 cm\"\nREWARD_ZONES = {\"A\": (80.0, 130.0), \"B\": (200.0, 250.0), \"C\": (320.0, 370.0)}\nZONE_NAMES = [\"A\", \"B\", \"C\"]\n\n# reward_relative/behavior.py :: get_reward_zones(..., change_trial=30)\n# Paper: \"Each switch occurred after 30 trials.\"\nCHANGE_TRIAL = 30\n\n# reward_relative/utilities.py :: default_dff_method\nNEU_COEF = 0.7                 # neuropil subtraction coefficient\nBASELINE_FILTER_LEN = 300      # maximin window, 300 samples @15.5 Hz ~= 19.3 s (\"20 s\")\nBASELINE_SMOOTH_SIGMA = 15     # pre-maximin Gaussian smoothing sigma (samples)\nDFF_SMOOTH_SIGMA = 2           # post-dF/F Gaussian smoothing sigma (~0.129 s)\n\n# Paper: putative interneurons excluded if corr(dF/F, speed) > 0.5\nSPEED_CORR_THRESH = 0.5\n\n# Paper: lick-sensor failure detection (~0.65% of trials, n = 81 / 12,376)\nLICK_ERR_COUNT_THRESH = 2      # cumulative lick count per imaging frame\nLICK_ERR_FRAC_THRESH = 0.3     # fraction of frames in the trial\n\nTRACK_LENGTH = 450.0           # cm\nPOS_BIN_EDGES = [90.0, 180.0, 270.0, 360.0]\nSPEED_BIN_EDGES = [2.0, 10.0, 20.0, 40.0]\n\nOUTPUT_NAMES = [\n    \"reward_zone_distance\",\n    \"position\",\n    \"speed\",\n    \"lick\",\n    \"reward_zone_location\",\n    \"reward_outcome\",\n]\nOUTPUT_VALUES = [\n    [\"< -50 cm\", \"-50 to -10 cm\", \"-10 to <0 cm\", \"0 cm (in reward zone)\",\n     \">0 to +10 cm\", \"+10 to +50 cm\", \"> +50 cm\"],\n    [\"< 90 cm\", \"90-180 cm\", \"180-270 cm\", \"270-360 cm\", \"> 360 cm\"],\n    [\"< 2 cm/s\", \"2-10 cm/s\", \"10-20 cm/s\", \"20-40 cm/s\", \"> 40 cm/s\"],\n    [\"no lick\", \"lick\"],\n    [\"zone A (80-130 cm)\", \"zone B (200-250 cm)\", \"zone C (320-370 cm)\"],\n    [\"omitted\", \"rewarded\"],\n]\nINPUT_NAMES = [\n    \"time_from_trial_start_s\",\n    \"environment\",          # 0 = ENV1, 1 = ENV2\n    \"trial_number\",\n    \"previous_trial_reward\",\n]\n\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# Helpers copied / adapted from the reference code\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\ndef nansmooth(a, sig, axis=-1):\n    \"\"\"Gaussian smoothing that does not propagate NaNs.\n\n    Verbatim port of reward_relative/utilities.py :: nansmooth (and the\n    equivalent TwoPUtils.utilities.nansmooth used for the dF/F baseline).\n    \"\"\"\n    nan_inds = np.isnan(a)\n    a_nanless = np.copy(a)\n    a_nanless[nan_inds] = 0\n    one = np.ones(a.shape)\n    one[nan_inds] = 0.001\n    a_nanless = sp.ndimage.gaussian_filter1d(a_nanless, sig, axis=axis)\n    one = sp.ndimage.gaussian_filter1d(one, sig, axis=axis)\n    return a_nanless / one\n\n\ndef compute_dff(F, Fneu, trial_starts, trial_ends):\n    \"\"\"Compute dF/F exactly as in reward_relative/preprocessing.py :: dff().\n\n    Steps (keep_teleports=False, neuropil_method='subtract',\n    baseline_method='maximin', subtract_baseline=True):\n      1. keep only within-trial samples (everything else NaN)\n      2. F  <- F - 0.7 * Fneu\n      3. add back 0.7 * <Fneu> of the trial so dF/F is close to true dF/F\n      4. baseline = maximin (min then max filter, 300 samples ~ 20 s) of the\n         Gaussian-smoothed (sigma 15) signal, computed within each trial\n      5. dF/F = (F - baseline) / |baseline|\n      6. smooth dF/F with a 2-sample s.d. Gaussian kernel, within each trial\n\n    Args:\n        F:     (n_neurons, n_frames) ROI fluorescence\n        Fneu:  (n_neurons, n_frames) neuropil fluorescence\n        trial_starts, trial_ends: frame indices, trial i spans [start, end)\n\n    Returns:\n        dff: (n_neurons, n_frames) with NaN outside of trials\n        in_trial: boolean (n_frames,) mask of within-trial samples\n    \"\"\"\n    f_ = np.full(F.shape, np.nan)\n    fneu_ = np.full(F.shape, np.nan)\n    for s, e in zip(trial_starts, trial_ends):\n        f_[:, s:e] = F[:, s:e]\n        fneu_[:, s:e] = Fneu[:, s:e]\n\n    in_trial = ~np.isnan(f_[0, :])\n\n    # neuropil subtraction\n    f_ -= NEU_COEF * fneu_\n\n    flow = np.full(F.shape, np.nan)\n    for s, e in zip(trial_starts, trial_ends):\n        # add back the trial-mean neuropil so we never divide by a small number\n        f_[:, s:e] = f_[:, s:e] + NEU_COEF * np.nanmean(fneu_[:, s:e], axis=1,\n                                                        keepdims=True)\n        tmp = nansmooth(f_[:, s:e], BASELINE_SMOOTH_SIGMA, axis=1)\n        tmp = sp.ndimage.minimum_filter1d(tmp, BASELINE_FILTER_LEN, axis=-1)\n        flow[:, s:e] = sp.ndimage.maximum_filter1d(tmp, BASELINE_FILTER_LEN, axis=-1)\n\n    dff = np.full(F.shape, np.nan)\n    dff[:, in_trial] = ((f_[:, in_trial] - flow[:, in_trial])\n                        / np.abs(flow[:, in_trial]))\n\n    for s, e in zip(trial_starts, trial_ends):\n        dff[:, s:e] = nansmooth(dff[:, s:e], DFF_SMOOTH_SIGMA, axis=1)\n\n    return dff, in_trial\n\n\ndef reward_zone_labels_from_scene(scene, n_trials, change_trial=CHANGE_TRIAL):\n    \"\"\"Per-trial reward-zone label, following behavior.py :: get_reward_zones().\n\n    Scene strings look like 'Env1_LocationA'          (no switch)\n                            'Env1_LocationB_to_A'      (zone switch)\n                            'Env1_B_to_Env2_C'         (zone + environment switch)\n    The zone switches after `change_trial` trials.\n\n    Returns list of 'A'/'B'/'C' of length n_trials.\n    \"\"\"\n    # no-switch scenes: Env{1,2,3}_Location{A,B,C}\n    for z in ZONE_NAMES:\n        if scene.endswith(\"_Location\" + z):\n            return [z] * n_trials\n\n    # switch scenes: '<zone0>_to' ... ends with '<zone1>'\n    zone1 = scene[-1]\n    zone0 = None\n    for z in ZONE_NAMES:\n        if (z + \"_to\") in scene:\n            zone0 = z\n            break\n    if zone0 is None or zone1 not in ZONE_NAMES:\n        raise NotImplementedError(f\"Cannot parse reward zones for scene '{scene}'\")\n    return [zone0] * min(change_trial, n_trials) + \\\n           [zone1] * max(n_trials - change_trial, 0)\n\n\ndef discretize_reward_distance(pos, zone_start, zone_end):\n    \"\"\"Signed distance to the nearest point of the reward zone -> 7 classes.\n\n    d < 0   : animal is before the zone (d = pos - zone_start)\n    d = 0   : animal is inside the zone\n    d > 0   : animal is past the zone (d = pos - zone_end)\n    \"\"\"\n    d = np.zeros_like(pos)\n    before = pos < zone_start\n    after = pos > zone_end\n    d[before] = pos[before] - zone_start\n    d[after] = pos[after] - zone_end\n\n    out = np.full(pos.shape, 3, dtype=np.int64)     # 3 == inside the zone\n    out[d < -50.0] = 0\n    out[(d >= -50.0) & (d < -10.0)] = 1\n    out[(d >= -10.0) & (d < 0.0)] = 2\n    out[(d > 0.0) & (d <= 10.0)] = 4\n    out[(d > 10.0) & (d <= 50.0)] = 5\n    out[d > 50.0] = 6\n    return out, d\n\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# Session processing\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\ndef load_session(path):\n    \"\"\"Read everything we need out of one NWB file using pynwb.\"\"\"\n    from pynwb import NWBHDF5IO\n\n    with NWBHDF5IO(path, \"r\", load_namespaces=True) as io:\n        nwb = io.read()\n\n        subject = nwb.subject.subject_id\n        session_id = nwb.session_id\n        # nwb.identifier == '/data/<...>/<animal>/<date>/<scene>'\n        ident_parts = nwb.identifier.rstrip(\"/\").split(\"/\")\n        scene = ident_parts[-1]\n        date = ident_parts[-2]\n\n        ophys = nwb.processing[\"ophys\"]\n        plane_seg = (ophys.data_interfaces[\"ImageSegmentation\"]\n                     .plane_segmentations[\"PlaneSegmentation\"])\n        # suite2p iscell.npy, column 0 == (manually curated) cell/not-cell label\n        iscell = np.asarray(plane_seg[\"iscell\"].data)[:, 0].astype(bool)\n        plane_idx_all = np.asarray(plane_seg[\"planeIdx\"].data).astype(int)\n\n        # Multi-plane animals (m17, m18) store one RoiResponseSeries per imaging\n        # plane; the PlaneSegmentation table concatenates the planes' ROIs. Planes\n        # are pooled, as in the paper (\"planes were pooled for all analyses except\n        # those in Extended Data Fig. 7\").\n        fluo = ophys.data_interfaces[\"Fluorescence\"].roi_response_series\n        neuro = ophys.data_interfaces[\"Neuropil\"].roi_response_series\n        plane_keys = sorted(fluo.keys(), key=lambda k: int(k.replace(\"plane\", \"\")))\n        n_roi_total = len(plane_idx_all)\n        n_frames = fluo[plane_keys[0]].data.shape[0]\n        F = np.empty((n_roi_total, n_frames), dtype=np.float64)\n        Fneu = np.empty((n_roi_total, n_frames), dtype=np.float64)\n        for k in plane_keys:\n            p = int(k.replace(\"plane\", \"\"))\n            rows = np.where(plane_idx_all == p)[0]\n            assert len(rows) == fluo[k].data.shape[1], (\n                f\"{k}: {len(rows)} ROIs in table vs {fluo[k].data.shape[1]} traces\")\n            F[rows] = np.asarray(fluo[k].data[:]).T\n            Fneu[rows] = np.asarray(neuro[k].data[:]).T\n        rate = float(fluo[plane_keys[0]].rate)\n        plane_idx = plane_idx_all[iscell]\n        F = F[iscell]\n        Fneu = Fneu[iscell]\n\n        bts = (nwb.processing[\"behavior\"]\n               .data_interfaces[\"BehavioralTimeSeries\"].time_series)\n        beh = {k: np.asarray(v.data[:]) for k, v in bts.items() if k != \"Reward\"}\n        timestamps = np.asarray(bts[\"position\"].timestamps[:])\n        reward_times = np.asarray(bts[\"Reward\"].timestamps[:])\n\n    n_planes = int(plane_idx_all.max()) + 1\n\n    # In 10 of the 2-plane sessions the fluorescence series has exactly one frame\n    # more than the VR-aligned behaviour: TwoPUtils' vr_align_to_2P builds\n    # int(max_idx / n_planes) rows, which truncates the final volume. The extra\n    # frame is at the end of the recording, so drop it.\n    n_extra = F.shape[1] - len(timestamps)\n    assert 0 <= n_extra <= 1, (\n        f\"fluorescence has {F.shape[1]} frames, behaviour {len(timestamps)}\")\n    if n_extra:\n        F = F[:, :len(timestamps)]\n        Fneu = Fneu[:, :len(timestamps)]\n    return dict(subject=subject, session_id=session_id, scene=scene, date=date,\n                iscell=iscell, plane_idx=plane_idx, F=F, Fneu=Fneu, rate=rate,\n                beh=beh, timestamps=timestamps, reward_times=reward_times,\n                n_planes=n_planes, n_roi_total=int(n_roi_total))\n\n\ndef process_session(path, show_processing=False, plot_prefix=None, verbose=True):\n    \"\"\"Convert one NWB session into (neural, input, output, info) lists.\"\"\"\n    t_load = time.time()\n    S = load_session(path)\n    t_load = time.time() - t_load\n\n    t_proc = time.time()\n    beh = S[\"beh\"]\n    ts = S[\"timestamps\"]\n    pos = beh[\"position\"]\n    speed = beh[\"speed\"]\n    lick = beh[\"lick\"]\n    env_ts = beh[\"environment\"]\n    rzone_ts = beh[\"reward_zone\"]\n    scanning = beh[\"scanning\"]\n    trial_num_ts = beh[\"trial number\"]\n\n    starts = np.where(beh[\"trial_start\"] > 0)[0]\n    ends = np.where(beh[\"teleport\"] > 0)[0]\n    assert len(starts) == len(ends), \"trial_start / teleport count mismatch\"\n    assert np.all(ends > starts), \"teleport must follow its trial_start\"\n    n_trials_raw = len(starts)\n\n    # ---------------- neural: dF/F, then interneuron exclusion --------------- #\n    dff, in_trial = compute_dff(S[\"F\"], S[\"Fneu\"], starts, ends)\n\n    # Paper: \"Additional putative interneurons were detected for exclusion ...\n    #         by a Pearson correlation of >0.5 between their dF/F timeseries and\n    #         the animal's running speed\"\n    d = dff[:, in_trial]\n    sp_ = speed[in_trial]\n    d0 = d - d.mean(axis=1, keepdims=True)\n    s0 = sp_ - sp_.mean()\n    denom = np.sqrt((d0 ** 2).sum(axis=1) * (s0 ** 2).sum())\n    with np.errstate(invalid=\"ignore\", divide=\"ignore\"):\n        speed_corr = (d0 @ s0) / denom\n    speed_corr = np.nan_to_num(speed_corr, nan=0.0)\n    keep_cells = speed_corr <= SPEED_CORR_THRESH\n    n_interneuron = int((~keep_cells).sum())\n    dff_all = dff\n    dff = dff[keep_cells]\n    plane_idx = S[\"plane_idx\"][keep_cells]\n    n_neurons = dff.shape[0]\n\n    # ---------------- per-trial behavioural variables ------------------------ #\n    # reward delivery: the 'Reward' TimeSeries holds one timestamp per delivery\n    rew_frames = np.searchsorted(ts, S[\"reward_times\"])\n\n    zone_labels = reward_zone_labels_from_scene(S[\"scene\"], n_trials_raw)\n\n    trial_rewarded = np.zeros(n_trials_raw, dtype=np.int64)\n    trial_env = np.zeros(n_trials_raw, dtype=np.int64)\n    trial_lick_error = np.zeros(n_trials_raw, dtype=bool)\n    trial_unscanned = np.zeros(n_trials_raw, dtype=bool)\n    observed_zone = np.full(n_trials_raw, -1, dtype=np.int64)\n\n    for i, (s, e) in enumerate(zip(starts, ends)):\n        # reference behavior.py :: get_trial_types\n        #   isreward = any(reward > 0) and any(rzone > 0) within the trial\n        in_zone = rzone_ts[s:e] > 0\n        got_reward = np.any((rew_frames >= s) & (rew_frames < e))\n        trial_rewarded[i] = int(got_reward and np.any(in_zone))\n\n        ev = np.unique(env_ts[s:e])\n        ev = ev[ev >= 0]\n        trial_env[i] = int(ev[0]) if ev.size else -1\n\n        # lick-sensor failure, exactly as described in the paper\n        trial_lick_error[i] = (np.mean(lick[s:e] > LICK_ERR_COUNT_THRESH)\n                               > LICK_ERR_FRAC_THRESH)\n        trial_unscanned[i] = np.any(scanning[s:e] < 0)\n\n        if np.any(in_zone):\n            p = pos[s:e][np.where(in_zone)[0][0]]\n            observed_zone[i] = int(np.argmin([abs(p - REWARD_ZONES[z][0])\n                                              for z in ZONE_NAMES]))\n\n    # sanity check: where the animal actually triggered the reward zone, the\n    # zone derived from the scene name must agree\n    scene_zone = np.array([ZONE_NAMES.index(z) for z in zone_labels])\n    seen = observed_zone >= 0\n    n_zone_mismatch = int(np.sum(observed_zone[seen] != scene_zone[seen]))\n\n    # previous-trial outcome. Trial 0 of a session is preceded (within minutes)\n    # by ~30 rewarded warm-up trials of the same task, so it is assigned 1.\n    prev_rewarded = np.empty(n_trials_raw, dtype=np.int64)\n    prev_rewarded[0] = 1\n    prev_rewarded[1:] = trial_rewarded[:-1]\n\n    # ---------------- trial curation ---------------------------------------- #\n    trial_len = ends - starts\n    too_short = trial_len < 2\n    keep_trial = ~(trial_lick_error | trial_unscanned | too_short)\n\n    # ---------------- build per-trial arrays -------------------------------- #\n    neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials = [], [], []\n    kept_idx = []\n    for i in np.where(keep_trial)[0]:\n        s, e = starts[i], ends[i]\n        T = e - s\n\n        neural_trials.append(np.ascontiguousarray(dff[:, s:e], dtype=np.float32))\n\n        inp = np.empty((4, T), dtype=np.float32)\n        inp[0] = ts[s:e] - ts[s]           # time from trial start (s)\n        inp[1] = trial_env[i]              # 0 = ENV1, 1 = ENV2\n        inp[2] = i                         # trial number within session\n        inp[3] = prev_rewarded[i]\n        input_trials.append(inp)\n\n        zs, ze = REWARD_ZONES[zone_labels[i]]\n        p = pos[s:e]\n        out = np.empty((6, T), dtype=np.int64)\n        out[0], _ = discretize_reward_distance(p, zs, ze)\n        out[1] = np.clip(np.digitize(p, POS_BIN_EDGES), 0, 4)\n        out[2] = np.clip(np.digitize(speed[s:e], SPEED_BIN_EDGES), 0, 4)\n        out[3] = (lick[s:e] > 0).astype(np.int64)\n        out[4] = scene_zone[i]\n        out[5] = trial_rewarded[i]\n        output_trials.append(out)\n        kept_idx.append(int(i))\n\n    info = dict(\n        file=os.path.basename(path), subject=S[\"subject\"], session_id=S[\"session_id\"],\n        exp_day=int(S[\"session_id\"]), scene=S[\"scene\"], date=S[\"date\"],\n        n_roi_total=S[\"n_roi_total\"], n_iscell=int(S[\"iscell\"].sum()),\n        n_interneuron_excluded=n_interneuron, n_neurons=n_neurons,\n        n_planes=S[\"n_planes\"], rate=S[\"rate\"],\n        n_trials_raw=n_trials_raw, n_trials_kept=len(kept_idx),\n        n_lick_error=int(trial_lick_error.sum()),\n        n_unscanned=int(trial_unscanned.sum()), n_too_short=int(too_short.sum()),\n        n_zone_mismatch=n_zone_mismatch, n_zone_observed=int(seen.sum()),\n        frac_rewarded=float(trial_rewarded.mean()),\n        kept_trial_idx=kept_idx,\n        zone_labels=[zone_labels[i] for i in kept_idx],\n        dt=float(np.median(np.diff(ts))),\n    )\n    t_proc = time.time() - t_proc\n    info[\"t_load\"] = t_load\n    info[\"t_proc\"] = t_proc\n\n    if verbose:\n        print(f\"  {info['file']}: {n_neurons} neurons \"\n              f\"({info['n_iscell']} iscell - {n_interneuron} interneuron), \"\n              f\"{len(kept_idx)}/{n_trials_raw} trials, scene={S['scene']}, \"\n              f\"zone-mismatch={n_zone_mismatch}/{info['n_zone_observed']}, \"\n              f\"load={t_load:.1f}s proc={t_proc:.1f}s\", flush=True)\n\n    if show_processing:\n        make_processing_plots(S, dff_all, keep_cells, speed_corr, starts, ends,\n                              zone_labels, scene_zone, trial_rewarded, trial_env,\n                              neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials,\n                              kept_idx, plot_prefix)\n\n    return neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials, plane_idx, info\n\n\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# Diagnostic plots\n# ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\ndef make_processing_plots(S, dff, keep_cells, speed_corr, starts, ends,\n                          zone_labels, scene_zone, trial_rewarded, trial_env,\n                          neural_trials, input_trials, output_trials,\n                          kept_idx, prefix):\n    import matplotlib\n    matplotlib.use(\"Agg\")\n    import matplotlib.pyplot as plt\n\n    beh, ts = S[\"beh\"], S[\"timestamps\"]\n    pos, speed, lick = beh[\"position\"], beh[\"speed\"], beh[\"lick\"]\n\n    ntr_show = min(6, len(starts))\n    i0, i1 = starts[0], ends[ntr_show - Second, each decision you make about how to load, filter, process, align, and save the data will be compared with the decision made by an expert. A decision that differs from the expert's is acceptable if it is equally well justified; decisions that are poorly justified will be flagged as concerning or incorrect.\n    24→\n    25→## Reference Information\n    26→\n    27→- The reference **paper** \"A flexible hippocampal population code for experience relative to reward\" is in the file `/app/paper.pdf`.\n    28→- Some parts of the paper that describe the experiment and processing have been copied to the file `/app/methods.txt`. \n    29→- **Code** from the paper are in the directory `/app/code`.\n    30→- **Data** from the paper are in the directory `/app/data`.\n    31→- **Documentation** on **NWB** file format is at https://nwb-schema.readthedocs.io/en/latest/. Documentation about `pynwb` is at https://pynwb.readthedocs.io/en/stable/. `pynwb` documentation is also downloaded to `/app/pynwb_docs/`.\n    32→\n    33→## Decoder Task\n    34→\n    35→Temporally align based on start of the trial. \n    36→\n    37→### Decoder Inputs:\n    38→- Time from start of trial in seconds (continuous, time-varying)\n    39→- Environment type (binary, ENV1 vs ENV2, per trial)\n    40→- Trial number (continuous, per trial)\n    41→- Previous trial outcome (binary, omitted = 0, rewarded = 1, per trial)\n    42→\n    43→### Decoder Outputs\n    44→- Distance to any location in the reward zone, time-varying. Discretization bins:\n    45→  - 0:  < -50 cm\n    46→  - 1: -50 to -10 cm\n    47→  - 2: -10 cm to < 0 cm\n    48→  - 3: 0 cm\n    49→  - 4: >0 cm to +10 cm\n    50→  - 5: +10 to +50 cm\n    51→  - 6: > +50 cm\n    52→- Absolute position in corridor, time-varying. Discretized into 5 equal-sized bins spanning the 450 cm track:\n    53→  - 0: < 90 cm\n    54→  - 1: 90 to 180 cm\n    55→  - 2: 180 to 270 cm\n    56→  - 3: 270 to 360 cm\n    57→  - 4: > 360 cm\n    58→- Speed, time-varying. Discretized into bins:\n    59→  - 0: < 2 cm/s\n    60→  - 1: 2-10 cm/s\n    61→  - 2: 10-20 cm/s\n    62→  - 3: 20-40 cm/s\n    63→  - 4: > 40 cm/s\n    64→- Lick, time-varying. 0 = no, 1 = yes\n    65→- Reward zone location, per-trial. 0 = A, 1 = B, 2 = C\n    66→- Reward outcome, per-trial. 0 = no, 1 = yes\n    67→\n    68→## Target Data Format\n    69→\n    70→- Save the full converted dataset to the pickle file `/app/converted_data.pkl`.\n    71→- All datasets must be converted into the following Python dictionary structure:\n    72→\n    73→```python\n    74→data = {\n    75→    'neural': [  # list of sessions\n    76→        [  # list of trials for session 1\n    77→            neuron_by_time_matrix,  # shape: (n_neurons, n_timepoints)\n    78→            neuron_by_time_matrix,  # trial 2\n    79→            ...\n    80→        ],\n    81→        [  # list of trials for session 2\n    82→            ...\n    83→        ],\n    84→        ...\n    85→    ],\n    86→\n    87→    'input': [  # list of sessions\n    88→        [  # list of trials for session 1\n    89→            input_data,  # stimulus/task variables, shape: (n_input, n_timepoints) or (n_input)\n    90→            ...\n    91→        ],\n    92→        ...\n    93→    ],\n    94→\n    95→    'output': [  # list of sessions\n    96→        [  # list of trials for session 1\n    97→            output_data,  # behavioral response, shape (n_output, n_timepoints) or (n_output)\n    98→            ...\n    99→        ],\n   100→        ...\n   101→    ],\n   102→\n   103→    'subjects': list of str, # ids of all subjects, length n_subjects\n   104→    'subject_idx': shape (n_sessions,), # index into subjects for each session\n   105→\n   106→    'brain_regions': list of str, # names of the brain regions recorded from, e.g. ALM, V1\n   107→    'brain_region_idx': [ # list of sessions\n   108→        neuron_region_idx, # shape: (n_neurons,)\n   109→    ], # index into `brain_regions` for each neuron\n   110→\n   111→    'input_names': list of str, # names for each input, of length n_input\n   112→    'output_names': list of str, # names of each output, of length n_output\n   113→    'output_values': [ # list of length n_output\n   114→        [ output_val0, ... ], # list of str of length d_output[0], name of each output value\n   115→        ...\n   116→    ],\n   117→\n   118→    'metadata': {\n   119→        'task_description': str, # description of output tasks\n   120→        'time_bin_size': float, # length of time bin in ms\n   121→        'temporal_alignment_event': str, # description of alignment event\n   122→        'off_start': float or None, # signed time from alignment event to start of trial in seconds, None if N/A\n   123→        'off_end': float or None, # signed time from alignment event to end of trial in seconds, None if N/A\n   124→        ...\n   125→    }\n   126→}\n   127→```\n   128→\n   129→### Key Format Requirements\n   130→\n   131→- **neural**: Neural activity data (e.g., firing rates, spike counts) organized by session and trial\n   132→  - Dimensions: (n_neurons, n_timepoints) for each trial.\n   133→  - Time bins should be the same size for all trials and sessions. \n   134→  - There needs to be at least two trials within each session in order to evaluate the decoder performance.\n   135→\n   136→- **input**: Variables that serve as inputs **to the decoder**\n   137→  - These are **decoder inputs**. They could be task inputs to the animal, but inputs to the animal can also be decoder outputs.\n   138→  - Examples: time from an aligning signal, stimulus characteristics, our animal behavior such as:\n   139→    - visual: contrast, position, orientation\n   140→    - audio: frequency, amplitude\n   141→    - behavior: running speed, pupil size\n   142→  - Dimensions: (d_input, n_timepoints) for each trial. \n   143→  - Should capture all relevant contextual information for the decoder\n   144→  - If an input is a time such as onset of some stimulus, represent it as a binary time series. \n   145→\n   146→- **output**: Variables to be decoded/predicted from neural activity\n   147→  - These are what we want the decoder to **predict**. They could be the animal's output behavior or properties of the stimulus.\n   148→  - Output variables must be **categorical**, i.e. discrete rather than continuous. If a variable is not discrete, follow instructions from Decoder Task to discretize it. \n   149→  - Examples: stimulus properties (contrast, orientation), choice (left/right), reaction time, position\n   150→  - Can be time-varying or discrete values per trial. If at all possible, make it time-varying\n   151→  - If an output is a time such as when a behavior occurred, represent it as a binary time series. \n   152→\n   153→- **subjects**: List of names of all unique subjects (mice).\n   154→- **subject_idx**: Index into subjects for each session.\n   155→  - Order should match order of sessions in `neural`, etc.\n   156→\n   157→- **brain_regions**: List of names of all brain regions recorded from.\n   158→- **brain_region_idx**: Index into brain_regions for each neuron in each session.\n   159→\n   160→- **input_names**: Names of each input variable.\n   161→  - Order should match order of `input` dimensions. \n   162→\n   163→- **output_names**: Names of each output variable.\n   164→  - Order should match order of `output` dimensions.\n   165→- **output_values**: Names of each output value for each output variable.\n   166→  - Order should match categorical `output` values.\n   167→\n   168→- **metadata**: Descriptive information. \n   169→  - `task_description`: Concise description of the task\n   170→  - `temporal_alignment_event`: Concise description of temporal alignment event\n   171→  - `off_start`: Signed time from alignment event to start of trial. Negative means before event, positive after. \n   172→  - `off_end`: Signed time from alignment event to end of trial\n   173→  - Add other relevant fields, e.g. `session_info`\n   174→\n   175→## Python environment\n   176→\n   177→- All required Python packages are already installed in the system Python environment.\n   178→- Use `python3` to run any python code.\n   179→- If you need to install additional libraries, use `pip install`.\n   180→\n   181→## Decoder Reference\n   182→\n   183→- Run this script with `python /app/train_decoder.py <data_file_path>` to validate.\n   184→- Optional arguments:\n   185→  - `--verify-only`: Only verify data format and print summary, then exit (does not train decoder).\n   186→  - `--plot-samples`: Plot sample trials from data and save to png files.\n   187→  - `--cpu`: Force torch to use CPU only (use if GPU runs out of memory).\n   188→- Pipe the output to the designated file so that the user can examine it.\n   189→\n   190→### Purpose\n   191→\n   192→These functions will be used during the validation phase to ensure:\n   193→1. Data structure is correct - will report errors and warnings to stdout. \n   194→2. Dimensions are consistent - will report errors and warnings to stdout. \n   195→3. Values are sensible - will report errors and warnings to stdout. \n   196→4. Decoder can successfully train and predict\n   197→5. Poor performance indicates data formatting issues that need investigation\n   198→\n   199→### Consistency requirements\n   200→\n   201→Your processing and formatting must **match the reference paper and code** with respect to:\n   202→- Loading of data\n   203→- Temporal alignment of different time series streams (neural, inputs, outputs)\n   204→- Processing of neural, input, and output data streams\n   205→- Curation of data: filtering of low-quality neurons, trials, sessions, and mice.\n   206→\n   207→To check consistency, you must compare statistics available in the reference paper and your converted dataset. \n   208→\n   209→Whenever possible, invent **SANITY CHECKS** that your loading and processing matches the reference paper and code. \n   210→\n   211→Discrepancies are only allowed if required by the Decoder Input and Decoder Output specifications above, or the task of training a neural decoder.\n   212→\n   213→Carefully verify your work after every step. When you find mistakes, document and fix them immediately. Be critical of results **after every step of processing**. \n   214→\n   215→You will be assessed on whether the decisions you make on how to load, filter, process, align, reformat, and save the data are reasonable and match the reference paper and code and these instructions. During testing, the reference version of `/app/train_decoder.py` will be run on your converted data. Data statistics and decoder accuracy will be compared to those achieved by human-written conversion code. \n   216→\n   217→---\n   218→\n   219→## Conversion Workflow\n   220→\n   221→**IMPORTANT**: Follow these steps in order. Each step has explicit completion criteria. Do not proceed to the next step until the current step is complete. Use the exact step names below when documenting progress in CONVERSION_NOTES.md.\n   222→\n   223→**STATUS TRACKING**: At the start of each step, update its status to `IN PROGRESS` in CONVERSION_NOTES.md. When complete, update to `COMPLETE`. This helps you and the user track progress.\n   224→\n   225→---\n   226→\n   227→### Step 0: Setup and Initialization\n   228→\n   229→**Goal**: Set up the working environment and create the notes file.\n   230→\n   231→**Actions**:\n   232→1. **FIRST**: Create `/app/CONVERSION_NOTES.md` with the template structure shown at the end of this document\n   233→2. Verify you can run `python3` and import key packages (numpy, torch)\n   234→3. List the contents of this directory to understand what files are available\n   235→\n   236→**Done when**: \n   237→- `/app/CONVERSION_NOTES.md` exists with the proper template structure\n   238→- You have confirmed Python and key packages work\n   239→- You have listed directory contents in CONVERSION_NOTES.md\n   240→\n   241→**⚠️ CHECKPOINT**: Before proceeding to Step 1, verify that `/app/CONVERSION_NOTES.md` exists by running `ls -la /app/CONVERSION_NOTES.md`. If it doesn't exist, STOP and create it now.\n   242→\n   243→---\n   244→\n   245→### Step 1: Reference Code Exploration\n   246→\n   247→**⚠️ CONSTRAINT REMINDER**: Only look at code in the `/app/code` directory. Do NOT look outside this project directory.\n   248→\n   249→**Goal**: Understand how the original authors loaded and processed their data.\n   250→\n   251→**Actions**:\n   252→- Look for documentation or README files in the `/app/code` directory\n   253→- Read the provided reference code bases to see how the reference paper loaded and manipulated the data\n   254→- Understand which functions are being called to read, curate, process, and manipulate the data\n   255→- For neural imaging data, does delta F over F need to be computed? \n   256→- For electrophysiology neural data, do cells need to be filtered based on quality? \n   257→- Note to file important functions, their inputs, and outputs\n   258→\n   259→**Done when**: You have documented key functions and their purposes in CONVERSION_NOTES.md under \"Step 1\".\n   260→\n   261→**⚠️ CHECKPOINT**: Before proceeding to Step 2, update Step 1 CONVERSION_NOTES.md.\n   262→\n   263→---\n   264→\n   265→### Step 2: Dataset Exploration\n   266→\n   267→**⚠️ CONSTRAINT REMINDER**: Only look at data in the `/app/data` directory. Do NOT look outside this project directory.\n   268→\n   269→**Goal**: Understand the native data structure\n   270→\n   271→**Actions**:\n   272→- Identify how data is organized (all files, formats, hierarchies)\n   273→- Determine available variables and their meanings\n   274→- Check data dimensions and data types\n   275→- Look for documentation or README files\n   276→- Note the data organization and structure\n   277→- Note dataset size parameters such as the **total number** of subjects (mice), sessions, trials, and neurons\n   278→\n   279→**Done when**: You have documented the data structure, file formats, size, and available variables in CONVERSION_NOTES.md under \"Step 2\".\n   280→\n   281→**⚠️ CHECKPOINT**: Before proceeding to Step 3, update Step 2 CONVERSION_NOTES.md.\n   282→\n   283→---\n   284→\n   285→### Step 3: Reference Text Reading\n   286→\n   287→**Goal**: Extract key information from the paper and methods.\n   288→\n   289→**Actions**:\n   290→- Scan the reference papers and methods.txt for important details about the data\n   291→- Look for and note to file:\n   292→  - Any information about dataset size (number of neurons, subjects, sessions, trials)\n   293→  - Fraction of data with different values, e.g. fraction of rewarded outcomes\n   294→  - Data processing, curation, and filtering\n   295→  - Variables important to experiments\n   296→  - Temporal alignment\n   297→  - Temporal binning\n   298→- Read the reference paper to find information about expected decoder accuracy.\n   299→\n   300→**Done when**: You have documented expected dataset statistics and processing details in CONVERSION_NOTES.md under \"Step 3\".\n   301→\n   302→**⚠️ CHECKPOINT**: Before proceeding to Step 4, update Step 3 CONVERSION_NOTES.md.\n   303→\n   304→---\n   305→\n   306→### Step 4: Check for Consistency\n   307→\n   308→**Goal**: Verify your understanding is consistent across all sources. \n   309→\n   310→**Actions**:\n   311→- Compare your understanding of:\n   312→  - (Follow Decoder Task section)\n   337→  - How to discretize continuous outputs (Follow Decoder Task section)\n   338→  - Which inputs/outputs should be **time-varying** vs **per-trial** (Follow Decoder Task section)\n   339→  - How does the reference code load and manipulate this data?\n   340→  - When possible, import or copy code from the reference code in `/app/code` directory. \n   341→- Consult CONVERSION_NOTES.md under Steps 1-4 and reference materials to determine:\n   342→  - Temporal binning for spike rates (if applicable)\n   343→  - Temporal alignment of trials\n   344→  - Data filtering and curation rules for neurons, trials, and sessions\n   345→  - Brain regions recorded from\n   346→- Mapping should be consistent with CONVERSION_NOTES.md under Steps 1-4.\n   347→- Check for variables indicating valid data periods — exclude invalid data\n   348→- Note all decisions, edge cases, and data quality issues\n   349→- Plan and document **sanity and consistency checks** based on dataset size information from the reference texts, code, and data\n   350→\n   351→**Done when**:\n   352→- You have a complete mapping document in CONVERSION_NOTES.md specifying every source-to-target variable mapping under \"Step 5\"\n   353→- You have documented all decisions under \"Step 5\"\n   354→- You have documented sanity and consistency checks under \"Step 5\"\n   355→\n   356→**⚠️ CHECKPOINT**: Before proceeding to Step 6, update Step 5 CONVERSION_NOTES.md.\n   357→\n   358→---\n   359→\n   360→### Step 6: Script Development\n   361→\n   362→**Goal**: Write the conversion script to `/app/convert_data.py`.\n   363→\n   364→**Actions**:\n   365→- Write all conversion code in the file `/app/convert_data.py`\n   366→- Script should run as **`python -u /app/convert_data.py <outpicklefile>`**\n   367→- Include options:\n   368→  - `--full` (default): Process all sessions\n   369→  - `--sample`: Process only 2 sessions for testing\n   370→  - `--show-processing`: Plot visualizations of EVERY processing step for up to 2 sessions. Save plots as `processing_<session_id>.png`.\n   371→- Reuse code from reference code bases where appropriate\n   372→- Use `pynwb` to interface with the data. Do not directly load and parse the `hdf5` files with `h5py`.\n   373→- Write efficient code:\n   374→  - Vectorize loops\n   375→  - Avoid unnecessary file I/O\n   376→  - Use parallel processing if beneficial\n   377→  - Print timing information to find bottlenecks\n   378→- Plots for `--show-processing` mode should visually convince the user that every step of the conversion is correct.\n   379→  - There are no temporal misalignments\n   380→  - Discretization of continuous outputs is correct\n   381→- Write clear, well-documented functions\n   382→- Handle missing data appropriately (consult references, use sensible defaults, document)\n   383→- Validate data shapes and types at each step\n   384→- Include sanity checks (e.g., trial counts match across arrays)\n   385→\n   386→**Done when**: `/app/convert_data.py` exists and runs efficiently without errors.\n   387→\n   388→**⚠️ DO NOT PROCEED** to Step 7 until `/app/convert_data.py` exists and you have updated Step 6 in CONVERSION_NOTES.md.\n   389→\n   390→---\n   391→\n   392→### Step 7: Sample Conversion and Validation\n   393→\n   394→**Goal**: Validate the conversion on a small sample before processing the full dataset.\n   395→\n   396→**Actions**:\n   397→1. Run `python -u /app/convert_data.py /app/sample_data.pkl --sample --show-processing 2>&1 | tee /app/conversion_sample_out.txt`\n   398→  a. Verify the sample data structure matches the specification.\n   399→  b. Check dimensions are consistent across trials/sessions.\n   400→  c. Check that no data is missed during conversion. \n   401→  d. Validate that metadata accurately describes the data.\n   402→2. Improve efficiency of conversion.\n   403→  a. Look at and note timing information for the conversion. \n   404→  b. Estimate how long the full conversion will run. When you do this, make sure that your estimate accounts for differences in lengths/numbers of trials/sessions. \n   405→  c. If full conversion time estimate is longer than 15 minutes, speed up the code by writing more efficient code (vectorizing loops, removing unnecessary or redundant computations) and/or including parallel processing. \n   406→3. Run `python -u /app/train_decoder.py /app/sample_data.pkl --verify-only 2>&1 | tee /app/verification_sample_out.txt`.\n   407→4. Analyze the output file `/app/verification_sample_out.txt`:\n   408→  a. Verify no errors reported.\n   409→  b. Attempt to address any warnings\n   410→  c. Check input ranges and output value distributions against expectations from reference texts.\n   411→\n   412→**Done when**: \n   413→- `/app/sample_data.pkl` is created and passes manual inspection\n   414→- `/app/verification_sample_out.txt` is created and passes inspection\n   415→- Processing plots show no anomalies\n   416→- Document sample statistics in CONVERSION_NOTES.md under \"Step 7\"\n   417→\n   418→**⚠️ DO NOT PROCEED** to Step 8 until `/app/sample_data.pkl` and `/app/verification_sample_out.txt` files exist, and CONVERSION_NOTES.md Step 7 is filled in. \n   419→\n   420→---\n   421→\n   422→### Step 8: Sample Decoder Training\n   423→\n   424→**Goal**: Verify the decoder runs successfully on sample data.\n   425→\n   426→**Actions**:\n   427→1. Run `python -u /app/train_decoder.py /app/sample_data.pkl 2>&1 | tee /app/train_decoder_sample_out.txt`\n   428→2. **Check training**: Verify loss decreases over epochs\n   429→3. **Check accuracy**: Verify accuracy is above chance for EVERY output\n   430→\n   431→**Investigate any issues**:\n   432→- If accuracy is low: check for conversion bugs or reconsider output representation\n   433→\n   434→**Done when**: \n   435→- Decoder training completes without errors\n   436→- Loss decreases over epochs\n   437→- Accuracies are above chance\n   438→- Document all results in CONVERSION_NOTES.md under \"Step 8\"\n   439→\n   440→**⚠️ DO NOT PROCEED** to Step 9 until `/app/train_decoder_sample_out.txt` file exists, and CONVERSION_NOTES.md Step 8 is filled in. \n   441→\n   442→---\n   443→\n   444→### Step 9: Full Conversion and Validation\n   445→\n   446→**Goal**: Process the complete dataset.\n   447→\n   448→**Actions**:\n   449→1. Review your time estimate from Step 7.\n   450→2. If estimated time is long (e.g. longer than 15 minutes), optimize bottlenecks first.\n   451→3. Run `python -u /app/convert_data.py /app/converted_data.pkl --full 2>&1 | tee /app/conversion_full_out.txt`\n   452→4. As the code runs, update your estimates of how long processing will take. If it is much longer than your previous estimate (> 1.5x), kill the process, optimize bottlenecks, and repeat. \n   453→5. Run `python -u /app/train_decoder.py /app/converted_data.pkl --verify-only 2>&1 | tee /app/verification_full_out.txt`. \n   454→6. Check that no data was lost during conversion \n   455→7. Spot-check a few sessions to verify data integrity\n   456→8. **Check for consistency** between dataset statistics in `/app/verification_full_out.txt` and the reference texts\n   457→9. Investigate any inconsistencies, and revise the conversion script until all dataset statistics are consistent\n   458→\n   459→**Done when**:\n   460→- `/app/converted_data.pkl` is created and passes manual inspection\n   461→- `/app/verification_full_out.txt` is created and passes inspection\n   462→- **ALL** dataset statistics are consistent with values from reference texts\n   463→- You have documented statistics and consistency in CONVERSION_NOTES.md under \"Step 9\"\n   464→\n   465→**⚠️ DO NOT PROCEED** to Step 10 until `/app/converted_data.pkl` and `/app/verification_full_out.txt` files exist and CONVERSION_NOTES.MD Step 9 is filled in.\n   466→\n   467→---\n   468→\n   469→### Step 10: Critical Review 1\n   470→\n   471→**Goal**: Find and fix any errors by performing the following checks. Fix issues and re-run affected steps. Iterate until no issues remain.\n   472→\n   473→**Check 1: Output log verification**\n   474→Read through `/app/verification_full_out.txt` to find and fix **all errors**. Address each warning by either fixing its cause or verifying it that it is not possible to fix it. Explain why each warning could not be fixed in `/app/CONVERSION_NOTES.md`. \n   475→\n   476→**Check 2: Construct sanity checks**\n   477→- Develop at least one sanity check each on neural, input, and output data.\n   478→- These must involve loading in the original data files (NOT through your conversion code).\n   479→- Construct comparison tests and criteria using `np.allclose()`. \n   480→- These tests can be spot-checks of the data, e.g. neural activity at trial 5, timepoint 10, neuron 3. \n   481→- Fix all mismatches/sanity check failures. \n   482→- Document your sanity checks to `/app/CONVERSION_NOTES.md`. \n   483→\n   484→**Check 3: Reference code comparison**\n   485→- Compare your code to the reference code and methods. \n   486→- For each major processing step — (a) data loading, (b) neuron/trial filtering, (c) temporal alignment, (d) binning, (e) input construction, (f) output construction — identify the corresponding code in your script and in the reference, and compare the logic. \n   487→- Check whether you use the same variables, filtering, and processing as the reference code. \n   488→- Fix any mismatches. \n   489→- Document your comparisons in `/app/CONVERSION_NOTES.md`. \n   490→- Explain your reasoning for any differences in `/app/CONVERSION_NOTES.md`.\n   491→\n   492→**Check 4: Key statistics comparison**\n   493→- Verify that key statistics (number of animals, sessions, trials, neurons, input and output ranges, output distributions) match information available in the reference paper. \n   494→- Write code to investigate any discrepancies. \n   495→- Fix any discovered issues. \n   496→- Document your comparisons in `/app/CONVERSION_NOTES.md`. \n   497→\n   498→**Check 5: Check for edge cases**\n   499→- Check for off-by-one errors at the beginning/ends of trials, sessions, and subjects, or in definitions of the inputs and outputs. \n   500→- There may be minor issues in the data that your code must handle. Check that your code robustly handles issues in the original data. \n   501→\n   502→**Iteration protocol**: If ANY check above reveals an issue:\n   503→1. Fix the issue in convert_data.py\n   504→2. Re-run the affected conversion and validation steps\n   505→3. Re-run ALL checks in this step (not just the one that failed)\n   506→4.
